Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Merge "Semantic Releases" into "Releases"

## Context

The dashboard currently has two separate concepts — "Releases" (source-level facts) and "Semantic Releases" (AI-generated project-level reports). This creates navigation clutter (two sidebar items, two separate pages) and cognitive overhead. Since every release can optionally have a semantic report, we merge them into a single "Releases" concept where each release row shows inline report status.

## Goal

- Remove the standalone "Semantic Releases" page and sidebar item
- Enrich each release row with its semantic report status (none / in-progress / completed)
- Show trigger/spinner/link inline on the Releases page and Projects page

---

## Step 1: Backend — Enrich Release model with semantic release info

### `internal/models/release.go`
Add 3 fields to `Release` struct:
```go
SemanticReleaseID     string `json:"semantic_release_id,omitempty"`
SemanticReleaseStatus string `json:"semantic_release_status,omitempty"`
SemanticReleaseUrgency string `json:"semantic_release_urgency,omitempty"`
```

### `internal/api/pgstore.go`
Modify all 4 release query methods to LEFT JOIN `semantic_releases` on `(project_id, version)`:

**`ListAllReleases`** (2 queries at lines 230-253): Add to each SELECT:
```sql
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT sr.id, sr.status, sr.report->>'urgency' AS urgency
    FROM semantic_releases sr
    WHERE sr.project_id = p.id AND sr.version = r.version
    ORDER BY sr.created_at DESC LIMIT 1
) sr_info ON true
```
Add columns: `COALESCE(sr_info.id::text,''), COALESCE(sr_info.status,''), COALESCE(sr_info.urgency,'')`
Add to Scan: `&rel.SemanticReleaseID, &rel.SemanticReleaseStatus, &rel.SemanticReleaseUrgency`

**`ListReleasesBySource`** (2 queries at lines 296-336): Same pattern.

**`ListReleasesByProject`** (2 queries at lines 363-404): Same pattern. Note: these use `JOIN` not `LEFT JOIN` for sources/projects, so `p.id` is always available.

**`GetRelease`** (line 407-422): Same pattern for the single-release query.

No handler changes needed — the enriched model serializes automatically.

---

## Step 2: Frontend types

### `web/lib/api/types.ts`
Add to `Release` interface:
```typescript
semantic_release_id?: string;
semantic_release_status?: string;
semantic_release_urgency?: string;
```

---

## Step 3: Releases page — Add "Report" column

### `web/app/releases/page.tsx`

1. Add imports: `Sparkles`, `Loader2`, `FileText` from lucide-react; `agent` from `@/lib/api/client`; `mutate` from `swr`
2. Add `"Report"` to the column headers array (between "Age" and the empty external-link column)
3. Add a `<td>` for each release row that renders:
   - **completed** → `<Link>` with FileText icon + "Report" text → `/projects/{pid}/semantic-releases/{srId}`
   - **pending/processing** → Loader2 spinner + status text
   - **no status / failed** → Sparkles button that calls `agent.triggerRun(projectId, version)` then revalidates SWR
4. Add SSE revalidation (the page doesn't currently have it) so report status updates appear in real-time

---

## Step 4: Projects page — Merge flow sections

### `web/app/projects/page.tsx`

**ProjectFlowCard:**
1. Remove the separate "Semantic Releases" FlowSection (lines 293-325)
2. In the "Releases" FlowSection, after each version chip, add an inline indicator:
   - Completed SR for that version → small FileText icon linking to report
   - Pending/processing SR → small Loader2 spinner
   - No SR → no indicator (keeps it clean; trigger is on the Releases page)
3. Keep the `srApi.list()` SWR fetch to provide the match data
4. Add `FileText`, `Loader2` to lucide-react imports

**ProjectCompactRow:**
1. Remove the separate "Latest semantic release" column
2. After the latest release version chip, show inline:
   - If matching SR completed → urgency badge + FileText link
   - If matching SR pending → spinner
   - If no SR → nothing (just the version chip as-is)

---

## Step 5: Remove "Semantic Releases" sidebar item

### `web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`
- Remove `{ href: "/semantic-releases", label: "Semantic Releases", icon: Brain }` from `navItems`
- Remove `Brain` from lucide-react imports

---

## Step 6: Redirect old Semantic Releases page

### `web/app/semantic-releases/page.tsx`
Replace with a redirect to `/releases`:
```tsx
"use client";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
export default function SemanticReleasesPage() {
  redirect("/releases");
}
```

**Keep unchanged:**
- `web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/[srId]/page.tsx` — detail page still works
- `web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx` — still used by detail page

---

     1→"use client";

     3→import { useState, Suspense } from "react";
     4→import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
     5→import useSWR from "swr";
     6→import Link from "next/link";
     7→import {
     8→  releases as releasesApi,
     9→  projects as projectsApi,
    10→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    11→import { ProviderBadge } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    12→import { VersionChip } from "@/components/ui/version-chip";

    14→import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";

    17→
    18→const PER_PAGE = 15;
    19→
    20→function getProviderUrl(
    21→  provider: string,
    22→  repository: string,
    23→  version: string
    24→): string | null {
    25→  switch (provider) {
    26→    case "github":
    27→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    28→    case "dockerhub":
    29→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    30→    case "ecr-public":
    31→      return `https://gallery.ecr.aws/${repository}`;
    32→    case "gitlab":
    33→      return `https://gitlab.com/${repository}/-/releases/${version}`;
    34→    default:
    35→      return null;
    36→  }
    37→}
    38→
    39→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    40→/*  Page                                                               */
    41→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    42→
    43→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    44→  return (
    45→    <Suspense>
    46→      <ReleasesPageInner />
    47→    </Suspense>
    48→  );
    49→}
    50→
    51→function ReleasesPageInner() {
    52→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    53→  const initialProject = searchParams.get("project") ?? "all";
    54→  const initialShowExcluded = searchParams.get("show_excluded") !== "false";
    55→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    56→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);
    57→  const [showExcluded, setShowExcluded] = useState(initialShowExcluded);
    58→
    59→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown */
    60→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", async () => {
    61→    const firstPage = await projectsApi.list(1, 100);
    62→    return firstPage;
    63→  });
    64→
    65→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */
    66→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    67→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter, showExcluded] : null,
    68→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)
    69→  );
    70→
    71→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    72→    projectFilter === "all" ? ["all-releases", page, showExcluded] : null,
    73→    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)
    74→  );
    75→
    76→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;
    77→
    78→  /* Releases come pre-enriched with project/source metadata from the backend */
    79→  const releases: Release[] =
    80→    projectFilter !== "all"
    81→      ? scopedData?.data ?? []
    82→      : allReleasesData?.data ?? [];
    83→
    84→  /* Pagination math */
    85→  const activeData = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedData : allReleasesData;
    86→  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;
    87→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));
    88→  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;
    89→  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);
    90→
    91→  return (
    92→    <div className="space-y-6">
    93→      {/* Page title */}
    94→      <h1
    95→        style={{
    96→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
    97→          fontSize: "24px",
    98→          fontWeight: 700,
    99→          color: "#111113",
   100→        }}
   101→      >
   102→        Releases
   103→      </h1>
   104→
   105→      {/* Filters */}
   106→      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   107→        <select
   108→          value={projectFilter}
   109→          onChange={(e) => {
   110→            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);
   111→            setPage(1);
   112→          }}
   113→          className="appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow"
   114→          style={{
   115→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   116→            fontSize: "13px",
   117→            color: "#111113",
   118→            border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   119→          }}
   120→          onFocus={(e) =>
   121→            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "0 0 0 2px #e8601a40")
   122→          }
   123→          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = "none")}
   124→        >
   125→          <option value="all">All Projects</option>
   126→          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (
   127→            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
   128→              {p.name}
   129→            </option>
   130→          ))}
   131→        </select>
   132→
   133→        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
   134→          <span style={{
   135→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   136→            fontSize: "13px",
   137→            color: "#6b7280",
   138→          }}>
   139→            Show excluded
   140→          </span>
   141→          <button
   142→            role="switch"
   143→            aria-checked={showExcluded}
   144→            onClick={() => {
   145→              const next = !showExcluded;
   146→              setShowExcluded(next);
   147→              setPage(1);
   148→              const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
   149→              if (next) params.delete("show_excluded");
   150→              else params.set("show_excluded", "false");
   151→              window.history.replaceState({}, "", `?${params.toString()}`);
   152→            }}
   153→            className="relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 items-center rounded-full transition-colors"
   154→            style={{ backgroundColor: showExcluded ? "#e8601a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   155→          >
   156→            <span
   157→              className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform"
   158→              style={{ transform: showExcluded ? "translateX(18px)" : "translateX(3px)" }}
   159→            />
   160→          </button>
   161→        </label>
   162→      </div>
   163→
   164→      {/* Table card */}
   165→      <div
   166→        className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white"
   167→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
   168→      >
   169→        {isLoading ? (
   170→          <div
   171→            className="py-16 text-center"
   172→            style={{
   173→              fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   174→              fontSize: "13px",
   175→              color: "#6b7280",
   176→            }}
   177→          >
   178→            Loading...
   179→          </div>
   180→        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (
   181→          <div className="py-16 text-center">
   182→            <p
   183→              style={{
   184→                fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   185→                fontStyle: "italic",
   186→                fontSize: "15px",
   187→                color: "#9ca3af",
   188→              }}
   189→            >
   190→              No releases ingested yet
   191→            </p>
   192→          </div>
   193→        ) : (
   194→          <table className="w-full">
   195→            <thead>
   196→              <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#fafaf9" }}>
   197→                {["Project", "Provider", "Repository", "Version", "Released", "Age", ""].map(
   198→                  (col) => (
   199→                    <th
   200→                      key={col}
   201→                      className="px-4 py-3 text-left"
   202→                      style={{
   203→                        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   204→                        fontSize: "11px",

   209→                      }}
   210→                    >
   211→                      {col}
   212→                    </th>
   213→                  )
   214→                )}
   215→              </tr>
   216→            </thead>
   217→            <tbody>
   218→              {releases.map((release) => (
   219→                <tr
   220→                  key={release.id}
   221→                  className={`transition-colors ${release.excluded ? '' : 'hover:bg-[#fafaf9]'}`}
   222→                  style={{
   223→                    borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5",

   225→                  }}
   226→                >
   227→                  {/* Project */}
   228→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   229→                    {release.project_id ? (
   230→                      <Link
   231→                        href={`/projects/${release.project_id}`}

   238→                        }}

   244→                        style={{
   245→                          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",

   249→                      >

   252→                    )}
   253→                  </td>
   254→
   255→                  {/* Provider */}
   256→                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
   257→                    {release.provider ? (
   258→                      <ProviderBadge provider={release.provider} />
   259→                    ) : (

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import Link from "next/link";
     5→import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
     6→import {
     7→  LayoutDashboard,
     8→  ListTodo,
     9→  FolderKanban,
    10→  Package,
    11→  Brain,
    12→  Bell,
    13→  Megaphone,
    14→  PanelLeftOpen,
    15→  PanelLeftClose,
    16→} from "lucide-react";
    17→import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    18→
    19→const navItems = [
    20→  { href: "/", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
    21→  { href: "/projects", label: "Projects", icon: FolderKanban },
    22→  { href: "/todo", label: "Todo", icon: ListTodo },
    23→  { href: "/releases", label: "Releases", icon: Package },
    24→  { href: "/semantic-releases", label: "Semantic Releases", icon: Brain },
    25→  { href: "/channels", label: "Channels", icon: Megaphone },
    26→  { href: "/subscriptions", label: "Subscriptions", icon: Bell },
    27→];
    28→
    29→export function Sidebar() {
    30→  const pathname = usePathname();
    31→  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
    32→
    33→  return (
    34→    <aside
    35→      className={cn(
    36→        "flex shrink-0 flex-col transition-[width] duration-200",
    37→        expanded ? "w-[200px]" : "w-[52px]"
    38→      )}
    39→      style={{ backgroundColor: "#16181c" }}
    40→    >
    41→      {/* Logo + toggle */}
    42→      <div className="flex h-12 items-center px-3">
    43→        {expanded ? (
    44→          <>
    45→            <img src="/logo.svg" alt="" className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0" />
    46→            <Link
    47→              href="/"
    48→              className="ml-2 text-[16px] italic text-white"
    49→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    50→            >
    51→              Changelogue
    52→            </Link>
    53→            <button
    54→              onClick={() => setExpanded(false)}
    55→              className="ml-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    56→              title="Collapse sidebar"
    57→            >
    58→              <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4" />
    59→            </button>
    60→          </>
    61→        ) : (
    62→          <button
    63→            onClick={() => setExpanded(true)}
    64→            className="mx-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    65→            title="Expand sidebar"
    66→          >
    67→            <PanelLeftOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
    68→          </button>
    69→        )}
    70→      </div>
    71→
    72→      {/* Nav */}
    73→      <nav className="flex-1 pt-2">
    74→        {navItems.map((item) => {
    75→          const isActive =
    76→            item.href === "/"
    77→              ? pathname === "/"
    78→              : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
    79→          return (
    80→            <Link
    81→              key={item.href}
    82→              href={item.href}
    83→              title={expanded ? undefined : item.label}
    84→              className={cn(
    85→                "flex items-center gap-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150",
    86→                expanded ? "pl-4 pr-3" : "justify-center px-0",
    87→                isActive
    88→                  ? "text-white"
    89→                  : "text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white"
    90→              )}
    91→              style={
    92→                isActive
    93→                  ? {
    94→                      borderLeft: "3px solid #e8601a",
    95→                      backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
    96→                      paddingLeft: expanded ? "13px" : undefined,
    97→                    }
    98→                  : { borderLeft: "3px solid transparent" }
    99→              }
   100→            >
   101→              <item.icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
   102→              {expanded && (
   103→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   104→                  {item.label}
   105→                </span>
   106→              )}
   107→            </Link>
   108→          );
   109→        })}
   110→      </nav>
   111→    </aside>
   112→  );
   113→}
   114→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
     4→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     7→import {
     8→  projects as projectsApi,
     9→  releases as releasesApi,
    10→  sources as sourcesApi,
    11→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    12→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    13→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    14→import { ProjectLogo } from "@/components/ui/project-logo";
    15→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    16→import { Plus, ArrowRight, LayoutGrid, List, Search, Pencil, ArrowUpDown } from "lucide-react";
    17→import { SourceForm } from "@/components/sources/source-form";
    18→import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
    19→import { ProjectForm } from "@/components/projects/project-form";
    20→import type { Project, Source } from "@/lib/api/types";
    21→
    22→const URGENCY_COLORS: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string }> = {
    23→  critical: { bg: "#dc2626", text: "#ffffff" },
    24→  high: { bg: "#f97316", text: "#ffffff" },
    25→  medium: { bg: "#f59e0b", text: "#ffffff" },
    26→  low: { bg: "#6b7280", text: "#ffffff" },
    27→};
    28→
    29→/* ---------- Project Card Logo ---------- */
    30→
    31→function ProjectCardLogo({ projectId, name }: { projectId: string; name: string }) {
    32→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
    33→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
    34→  );
    35→  return <ProjectLogo name={name} sources={data?.data} size={40} />;
    36→}
    37→
    38→/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */
    39→
    40→const LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)
    41→const MAX_LINES = 2;
    42→const MAX_HEIGHT = LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES;
    43→
    44→function FlowSection({
    45→  label,
    46→  moreHref,
    47→  children,
    48→}: {
    49→  label: string;
    50→  moreHref: string;
    51→  children: React.ReactNode;
    52→}) {
    53→  const measureRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    54→  const moreRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
    55→  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState<number | null>(null);
    56→
    57→  const items = React.Children.toArray(children);
    58→
    59→  const measure = useCallback(() => {
    60→    const el = measureRef.current;
    61→    if (!el) return;
    62→    const nodes = el.children;
    63→    const itemNodeCount = nodes.length - 2; // exclude label (first) and hidden "more…" span (last)
    64→    // Check if everything fits without overflow
    65→    if (el.scrollHeight <= MAX_HEIGHT + 4) {
    66→      setVisibleCount(items.length);
    67→      return;
    68→    }
    69→    // Measure actual "more…" link width
    70→    const moreWidth = moreRef.current ? moreRef.current.offsetWidth + 8 : 52;
    71→    const containerWidth = el.offsetWidth;
    72→    // First pass: find all items that fit within MAX_HEIGHT (2 lines)
    73→    let lastFitting = 0;
    74→    for (let i = 1; i <= itemNodeCount; i++) {
    75→      const node = nodes[i] as HTMLElement;
    76→      if (node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight > MAX_HEIGHT) break;
    77→      lastFitting = i;
    78→    }
    79→    // Second pass: walk backward from lastFitting until "more…" fits after the item
    80→    let count = lastFitting;
    81→    while (count > 1) {
    82→      const node = nodes[count] as HTMLElement;
    83→      const nodeBottom = node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight;
    84→      // If this item is on line 1, "more…" can always wrap to line 2 — we're fine
    85→      if (nodeBottom <= LINE_HEIGHT) break;
    86→      // Item is on line 2 — check if "more…" fits after it on the same line
    87→      const nodeRight = node.offsetLeft + node.offsetWidth;
    88→      if (nodeRight + moreWidth <= containerWidth) break;
    89→      count--;
    90→    }
    91→    setVisibleCount(Math.max(1, count));
    92→  }, [items.length]);
    93→
    94→  useEffect(() => {
    95→    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(measure);
    96→    const obs = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    97→    if (measureRef.current) obs.observe(measureRef.current);
    98→    return () => {
    99→      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
   100→      obs.disconnect();
   101→    };
   102→  }, [measure, items.length]);
   103→
   104→  const hasOverflow = visibleCount !== null && visibleCount < items.length;
   105→  const displayed = visibleCount !== null ? items.slice(0, visibleCount) : items;
   106→
   107→  return (
   108→    <div className="relative">
   109→      {/* Hidden measurer — renders all items to find cutoff */}
   110→      <div
   111→        ref={measureRef}
   112→        className="text-[13px] leading-7"
   113→        style={{ position: "absolute", visibility: "hidden", top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, pointerEvents: "none" }}
   114→        aria-hidden="true"
   115→      >
   116→        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5">
   117→          {label}:
   118→        </span>
   119→        {items}
   120→        {/* Hidden "more…" to measure its actual width */}
   121→        <span ref={moreRef} className="inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
   122→          more…
   123→        </span>
   124→      </div>
   125→      {/* Visible section */}
   126→      <div className="text-[13px] leading-7">
   127→        <span
   128→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5"
   129→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   130→        >
   131→          {label}:
   132→        </span>
   133→        {displayed}
   134→        {hasOverflow && (
   135→          <Link
   136→            href={moreHref}
   137→            className="inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
   138→            style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   139→          >
   140→            more…
   141→          </Link>
   142→        )}
   143→      </div>
   144→    </div>
   145→  );
   146→}
   147→
   148→/* ---------- Flow Card ---------- */
   149→
   150→function ProjectFlowCard({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   151→  const [sourceCreateOpen, setSourceCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   152→  const [editingSource, setEditingSource] = useState<Source | null>(null);
   153→
   154→  const { data: srcData, mutate: mutateSources } = useSWR(
   155→    `project-${project.id}-card-sources`,
   156→    () => sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id),
   157→  );
   158→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(
   159→    `project-${project.id}-card-releases`,
   160→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 25, true),
   161→  );
   162→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(
   163→    `project-${project.id}-card-sr`,
   164→    () => srApi.list(project.id, 1, 10),
   165→  );
   166→
   167→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   168→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   169→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   170→
   171→  const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
   172→    const m = new Map<string, Source>();
   173→    for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
   174→    return m;
   175→  }, [sources]);
   176→
   177→  return (
   178→    <div
   179→      className="rounded-md px-5 py-4"
   180→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   181→    >
   182→      {/* Header */}
   183→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
   184→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
   185→          <ProjectCardLogo projectId={project.id} name={project.name} />
   186→          <div className="min-w-0">
   187→            <Link
   188→              href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
   189→              className="group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[16px] font-bold transition-colors"
   190→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a" }}
   191→            >
   192→              {project.name}
   193→              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 opacity-0 -translate-x-1 transition-all group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:translate-x-0" />
   194→            </Link>
   195→            {project.description && (
   196→              <p
   197→                className="text-[13px] truncate"
   198→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   199→              >
   200→                {project.description}
   201→              </p>
   202→            )}
   203→          </div>
   204→        </div>
   205→      </div>
   206→
   207→      {/* Sources chips */}
   208→      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-3">
   209→        <span
   210→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-0.5"
   211→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   212→        >
   213→          Sources:
   214→        </span>
   215→        {sources.map((source) => (
   216→          <button
   217→            key={source.id}
   218→            onClick={() => setEditingSource(source)}
   219→            className="group/chip inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f0f0ee]"
   220→            style={{
   221→              backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   222→              border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   223→            }}
   224→          >
   225→            <span
   226→              className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
   227→              style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   228→            />
   229→            {(() => { const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider); return Icon ? <Icon size={12} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} /> : null; })()}
   230→            <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   231→              {source.repository}
   232→            </span>
   233→            {source.last_polled_at && (
   234→              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   235→                {timeAgo(source.last_polled_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   236→              </span>
   237→            )}
   238→            <Pencil className="h-2.5 w-2.5 hidden group-hover/chip:inline shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af", opacity: 0.5 }} />
   239→          </button>
   240→        ))}
   241→        <button
   242→          onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}
   243→          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium rounded border transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   244→          style={{
   245→            borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   246→            color: "#9ca3af",
   247→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   248→          }}
   249→        >
   250→          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   251→          Add Source
   252→        </button>
   253→      </div>
   254→
   255→      {/* Releases flow */}
   256→      {releases.length > 0 && (
   257→        <FlowSection label="Releases" moreHref={`/releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   258→          {releases.map((r) => {
   259→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   260→            return (
   261→              <span key={r.id} className="inline-flex items-baseline mr-2.5">
   262→                <Link
   263→                  href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   264→                  className={r.excluded ? "" : "text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"}
   265→                  style={{
   266→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   267→                    fontSize: "12px",
   268→                    ...(r.excluded ? { color: "#c4c4c0" } : {}),
   269→                  }}
   270→                >
   271→                  {r.version}
   272→                </Link>
   273→                {src && (
   274→                  <span
   275→                    className="text-[11px] ml-1 hidden sm:inline"
   276→                    style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#9ca3af" }}
   277→                  >
   278→                    ({src.repository.split("/").pop()})
   279→                  </span>
   280→                )}
   281→                <span
   282→                  className="text-[11px] ml-1"
   283→                  style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#c4c4c0" }}
   284→                >
   285→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   286→                </span>
   287→              </span>
   288→            );
   289→          })}
   290→        </FlowSection>
   291→      )}
   292→
   293→      {/* Semantic releases */}
   294→      {srItems.length > 0 && (
   295→        <FlowSection label="Semantic Releases" moreHref={`/semantic-releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   296→          {srItems.map((sr) => {
   297→            const urgencyStyle = sr.report?.urgency
   298→              ? URGENCY_COLORS[sr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   299→              : undefined;
   300→            return (
   301→              <span key={sr.id} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 mr-2.5">
   302→                <Link
   303→                  href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${sr.id}`}
   304→                  className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   305→                  style={{
   306→                    backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   307→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   308→                    color: "#1d4ed8",
   309→                  }}
   310→                >
   311→                  {sr.version}
   312→                </Link>
   313→                {urgencyStyle && (
   314→                  <span
   315→                    className="rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold tracking-wide leading-none"
   316→                    style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   317→                  >
   318→                    {sr.report?.urgency}
   319→                  </span>
   320→                )}
   321→              </span>
   322→            );
   323→          })}
   324→        </FlowSection>
   325→      )}
   326→
   327→      {/* Add Source dialog */}
   328→      <Dialog open={sourceCreateOpen} onOpenChange={setSourceCreateOpen}>
   329→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   330→          <DialogHeader>
   331→            <DialogTitle>Add Source</DialogTitle>
   332→          </DialogHeader>
   333→          <SourceForm
   334→            title="Add Source"
   335→            projectId={project.id}
   336→            onSubmit={async (input) => {
   337→              const res = await sourcesApi.create(project.id, input);
   338→              if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   339→            }}
   340→            onSuccess={() => {
   341→              setSourceCreateOpen(false);
   342→              mutateSources();
   343→            }}
   344→            onCancel={() => setSourceCreateOpen(false)}
   345→          />
   346→        </DialogContent>
   347→      </Dialog>
   348→
   349→      {/* Edit Source dialog */}
   350→      <Dialog
   351→        open={!!editingSource}
   352→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingSource(null); }}
   353→      >
   354→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   355→          <DialogHeader>
   356→            <DialogTitle>Edit Source</DialogTitle>
   357→          </DialogHeader>
   358→          {editingSource && (
   359→            <SourceForm
   360→              key={editingSource.id}
   361→              title="Edit Source"
   362→              initial={editingSource}
   363→              onSubmit={async (input) => {
   364→                await sourcesApi.update(editingSource.id, input);
   365→              }}
   366→              onSuccess={() => {
   367→                setEditingSource(null);
   368→                mutateSources();
   369→              }}
   370→              onCancel={() => setEditingSource(null)}
   371→            />
   372→          )}
   373→        </DialogContent>
   374→      </Dialog>
   375→    </div>
   376→  );
   377→}
   378→
   379→/* ---------- Compact Row ---------- */
   380→
   381→function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   382→  const router = useRouter();
   383→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-releases`, () =>
   384→    releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 5, true)
   385→  );
   386→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sources`, () =>
   387→    sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id)
   388→  );
   389→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sr`, () =>
   390→    srApi.list(project.id, 1, 3)
   391→  );
   392→
   393→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   394→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   395→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   396→  const latest = releases[0];
   397→  const latestSr = srItems[0];
   398→
   399→  const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
   400→    const m = new Map<string, Source>();
   401→    for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
   402→    return m;
   403→  }, [sources]);
   404→
   405→  const latestSrc = latest ? sourceMap.get(latest.source_id) : undefined;
   406→  const latestIcon = latestSrc ? getProviderIcon(latestSrc.provider) : undefined;
   407→
   408→  const urgencyStyle = latestSr?.report?.urgency
   409→    ? URGENCY_COLORS[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   410→    : undefined;
   411→
   412→  return (
   413→    <div
   414→      onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}
   415→      className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#f9f9f7] cursor-pointer"
   416→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   417→    >
   418→      {/* Project name */}
   419→      <span
   420→        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-bold shrink-0"
   421→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a", minWidth: "160px" }}
   422→      >
   423→        <ProjectLogo name={project.name} sources={sources} size={24} />
   424→        {project.name}
   425→      </span>
   426→
   427→      {/* Latest release */}
   428→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ minWidth: "140px" }}>
   429→        {latest ? (
   430→          <>
   431→            <Link
   432→              href={`/releases/${latest.id}`}
   433→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   434→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-300 transition-shadow"
   435→              style={{
   436→                backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   437→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   438→                color: "#374151",
   439→              }}
   440→            >
   441→              {latest.version}
   442→            </Link>
   443→            {latestIcon && latestIcon({ size: 12, className: "shrink-0", style: { color: "#9ca3af" } })}
   444→          </>
   445→        ) : (
   446→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   447→            No releases
   448→          </span>
   449→        )}
   450→      </span>
   451→
   452→      {/* Latest semantic release */}
   453→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   454→        {latestSr ? (
   455→          <>
   456→            <Link
   457→              href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   458→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   459→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none shrink-0 hover:ring-1 hover:ring-blue-300 transition-shadow"
   460→              style={{
   461→                backgroundColor: "#eff6ff",
   462→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   463→                color: "#1d4ed8",
   464→              }}
   465→            >
   466→              {latestSr.version}
   467→            </Link>
   468→            {urgencyStyle && (
   469→              <span
   470→                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none shrink-0"
   471→                style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   472→              >
   473→                {latestSr.report?.urgency}
   474→              </span>
   475→            )}
   476→            {latestSr.report?.summary && (
   477→              <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   478→                {latestSr.report.summary.length > 60
   479→                  ? latestSr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   480→                  : latestSr.report.summary}
   481→              </span>
   482→            )}
   483→          </>
   484→        ) : (
   485→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   486→            No analysis
   487→          </span>
   488→        )}
   489→      </span>
   490→
   491→      {/* Arrow */}
   492→      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }} />
   493→    </div>
   494→  );
   495→}
   496→
   497→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   498→
   499→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   500→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   501→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
   502→  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   503→  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<"updated" | "added" | "name">("updated");
   504→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
   505→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   506→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   507→
   508→  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
   509→  const filtered = search
   510→    ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
   511→    : items;
   512→  const sorted = useMemo(() => {
   513→    const arr = [...filtered];
   514→    switch (sortBy) {
   515→      case "updated":
   516→        return arr.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.updated_at).getTime() - new Date(a.updated_at).getTime());
   517→      case "added":
   518→        return arr.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime());
   519→      case "name":
   520→        return arr.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
   521→    }
   522→  }, [filtered, sortBy]);
   523→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   524→  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
   525→  const paged = sorted.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);
   526→
   527→  return (
   528→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   529→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   530→        <div>
   531→          <h1
   532→            style={{
   533→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   534→              fontSize: "24px",
   535→              fontWeight: 700,
   536→              color: "#111113",
   537→            }}
   538→          >
   539→            Projects
   540→          </h1>
   541→          <p
   542→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
   543→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   544→          >
   545→            Tracked software projects and their recent releases.
   546→          </p>
   547→        </div>
   548→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   549→          <div className="relative">
   550→            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   551→            <input
   552→              type="text"
   553→              value={search}
   554→              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
   555→              placeholder="Search projects..."
   556→              className="rounded-md border py-1.5 pl-8 pr-3 text-[13px] w-48"
   557→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   558→            />
   559→          </div>
   560→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[13px]" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}>
   561→            <ArrowUpDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   562→            <select
   563→              value={sortBy}
   564→              onChange={(e) => { setSortBy(e.target.value as "updated" | "added" | "name"); setPage(1); }}
   565→              className="bg-transparent outline-none cursor-pointer text-[13px]"
   566→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   567→            >
   568→              <option value="updated">Recently updated</option>
   569→              <option value="added">Recently added</option>
   570→              <option value="name">Name</option>
   571→            </select>
   572→          </div>
   573→          <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   574→            <button
   575→              onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
   576→              className="p-1.5 rounded-l-md transition-colors"
   577→              style={{
   578→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "cards" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   579→                color: viewMode === "cards" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   580→              }}
   581→              title="Card view"
   582→            >
   583→              <LayoutGrid className="h-4 w-4" />
   584→            </button>
   585→            <button
   586→              onClick={() => setViewMode("compact")}
   587→              className="p-1.5 rounded-r-md transition-colors"
   588→              style={{
   589→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "compact" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   590→                color: viewMode === "compact" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   591→              }}
   592→              title="Compact view"
   593→            >
   594→              <List className="h-4 w-4" />
   595→            </button>
   596→          </div>
   597→          <button
   598→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   599→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   600→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   601→          >
   602→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   603→            New Project
   604→          </button>
   605→        </div>
   606→      </div>
   607→
   608→      {isLoading ? (
   609→        <p
   610→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   611→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   612→        >
   613→          Loading...
   614→        </p>
   615→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   616→        <div
   617→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   618→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   619→        >
   620→          <p
   621→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   622→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   623→          >
   624→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   625→          </p>
   626→          <button
   627→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   628→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   629→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   630→          >
   631→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   632→            New Project
   633→          </button>
   634→        </div>
   635→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   636→        <p
   637→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   638→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   639→        >
   640→          No projects matching &ldquo;{search}&rdquo;
   641→        </p>
   642→      ) : (
   643→        <>
   644→          <div className={viewMode === "cards" ? "space-y-5" : "space-y-1.5"}>
   645→            {paged.map((project) =>
   646→              viewMode === "cards" ? (
   647→                <ProjectFlowCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   648→              ) : (
   649→                <ProjectCompactRow key={project.id} project={project} />
   650→              )
   651→            )}
   652→          </div>
   653→          {totalPages > 1 && (
   654→            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
   655→              <button
   656→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
   657→                disabled={currentPage <= 1}
   658→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   659→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   660→              >
   661→                Previous
   662→              </button>
   663→              <span
   664→                className="text-[13px]"
   665→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   666→              >
   667→                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
   668→              </span>
   669→              <button
   670→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
   671→                disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
   672→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   673→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   674→              >
   675→                Next
   676→              </button>
   677→            </div>
   678→          )}
   679→        </>
   680→      )}
   681→
   682→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   683→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   684→          <DialogHeader>
   685→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   686→          </DialogHeader>
   687→          <ProjectForm
   688→            title="Create Project"
   689→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   690→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   691→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   692→                const res = await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   693→                if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   694→              }
   695→            }}
   696→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   697→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   698→          />
   699→        </DialogContent>
   700→      </Dialog>
   701→    </div>
   702→  );
   703→}
   704→

    18→// PgStore implements all store interfaces using a PostgreSQL connection pool.
    19→type PgStore struct {
    20→	pool  *pgxpool.Pool
    21→	river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
    22→}
    23→
    24→// NewPgStore returns a new PgStore backed by the given connection pool and River client.
    25→// The river client may be nil if agent triggering is not needed.
    26→func NewPgStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool, riverClient *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) *PgStore {
    27→	return &PgStore{pool: pool, river: riverClient}
    28→}
    29→
    30→// SetRiverClient updates the River client on an existing PgStore.
    31→// This is used during bootstrap to inject the client after worker registration.
    32→func (s *PgStore) SetRiverClient(rc *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) {
    33→	s.river = rc
    34→}
    35→
    36→// --- ProjectsStore ---
    37→

    33→  if (!res.ok) {

    36→  }
    37→  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
    38→  return res.json();
    39→}

   389→			 ORDER BY COALESCE(r.released_at, r.created_at) DESC LIMIT $2 OFFSET $3`, projectID, perPage, offset)
   390→	}
   391→	if err != nil {
   392→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list releases by project: %w", err)
   393→	}
   394→	defer rows.Close()
   395→	var releases []models.Release
   396→	for rows.Next() {
   397→		var rel models.Release
   398→		if err := rows.Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,
   399→			&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository, &rel.Excluded); err != nil {
   400→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan release: %w", err)
   401→		}
   402→		releases = append(releases, rel)
   403→	}
   404→	return releases, total, nil
   405→}
   406→
   407→func (s *PgStore) GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error) {
   408→	var rel models.Release
   409→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   410→		`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,
   411→		        COALESCE(p.id::text,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,'')
   412→		 FROM releases r
   413→		 LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id
   414→		 LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
   415→		 WHERE r.id = $1`, id,
   416→	).Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,
   417→		&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository)
   418→	if err != nil {
   419→		return nil, err
   420→	}
   421→	return &rel, nil
   422→}
   423→
   424→// --- SubscriptionsStore ---
   425→
   426→func (s *PgStore) ListSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Subscription, int, error) {
   427→	var total int
   428→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT COUNT(*) FROM subscriptions`).Scan(&total)
   429→	if err != nil {
   430→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("count subscriptions: %w", err)
   431→	}
   432→	offset := (page - 1) * perPage
   433→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx,
   434→		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
   435→		 FROM subscriptions ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2`, perPage, offset)
   436→	if err != nil {
   437→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("list subscriptions: %w", err)
   438→	}
   439→	defer rows.Close()
   440→	var subs []models.Subscription
   441→	for rows.Next() {
   442→		var sub models.Subscription
   443→		if err := rows.Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
   444→			&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt); err != nil {
   445→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("scan subscription: %w", err)
   446→		}
   447→		subs = append(subs, sub)
   448→	}
   449→	return subs, total, nil

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useEffect, useCallback, Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport useSWR, { mutate } from \"swr\";\nimport Link from \"next/link\";\nimport {\n  releases as releasesApi,\n  projects as projectsApi,\n  agent,\n} from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport { ProviderBadge } from \"@/components/ui/provider-badge\";\nimport { VersionChip } from \"@/components/ui/version-chip\";\nimport type { Release, Project } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\nimport { ExternalLink, Sparkles, Loader2, FileText } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nimport { timeAgo } from \"@/lib/format\";\n\nconst PER_PAGE = 15;\nconst SSE_BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || \"/api/v1\";\n\nfunction getProviderUrl(\n  provider: string,\n  repository: string,\n  version: string\n): string | null {\n  switch (provider) {\n    case \"github\":\n      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;\n    case \"dockerhub\":\n      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;\n    case \"ecr-public\":\n      return `https://gallery.ecr.aws/${repository}`;\n    case \"gitlab\":\n      return `https://gitlab.com/${repository}/-/releases/${version}`;\n    default:\n      return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/*  Page                                                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nexport default function ReleasesPage() {\n  return (\n    <Suspense>\n      <ReleasesPageInner />\n    </Suspense>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ReleasesPageInner() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const initialProject = searchParams.get(\"project\") ?? \"all\";\n  const initialShowExcluded = searchParams.get(\"show_excluded\") !== \"false\";\n  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);\n  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);\n  const [showExcluded, setShowExcluded] = useState(initialShowExcluded);\n  const [triggeringVersion, setTriggeringVersion] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown */\n  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(\"projects-for-filter\", async () => {\n    const firstPage = await projectsApi.list(1, 100);\n    return firstPage;\n  });\n\n  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */\n  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(\n    projectFilter !== \"all\" ? [\"releases\", page, projectFilter, showExcluded] : null,\n    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)\n  );\n\n  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(\n    projectFilter === \"all\" ? [\"all-releases\", page, showExcluded] : null,\n    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)\n  );\n\n  const isLoading = projectFilter !== \"all\" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;\n\n  /* Releases come pre-enriched with project/source metadata from the backend */\n  const releases: Release[] =\n    projectFilter !== \"all\"\n      ? scopedData?.data ?? []\n      : allReleasesData?.data ?? [];\n\n  /* Pagination math */\n  const activeData = projectFilter !== \"all\" ? scopedData : allReleasesData;\n  const total = activeData?.meta?.total ?? 0;\n  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE));\n  const startRow = (page - 1) * PER_PAGE + 1;\n  const endRow = Math.min(page * PER_PAGE, total);\n\n  /* SSE revalidation — refresh on semantic_release events */\n  const revalidateReleases = useCallback(() => {\n    mutate((key) => {\n      if (Array.isArray(key)) {\n        return key[0] === \"releases\" || key[0] === \"all-releases\";\n      }\n      return false;\n    }, undefined, { revalidate: true });\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let es: EventSource | null = null;\n    let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\n\n    function connect() {\n      try {\n        es = new EventSource(`${SSE_BASE}/events`);\n        es.onmessage = (event) => {\n          try {\n            const parsed = JSON.parse(event.data);\n            if (parsed.type === \"semantic_release\") {\n              revalidateReleases();\n            }\n          } catch {\n            // ignore parse errors\n          }\n        };\n        es.onerror = () => {\n          es?.close();\n          retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, 5000);\n        };\n      } catch {\n        retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, 5000);\n      }\n    }\n\n    connect();\n    return () => {\n      es?.close();\n      if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);\n    };\n  }, [revalidateReleases]);\n\n  /* Trigger agent run */\n  const handleTrigger = async (projectId: string, version: string) => {\n    setTriggeringVersion(version);\n    try {\n      await agent.triggerRun(projectId, version);\n      revalidateReleases();\n    } catch {\n      // ignore errors\n    } finally {\n      setTriggeringVersion(null);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      {/* Page title */}\n      <h1\n        style={{\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n          fontSize: \"24px\",\n          fontWeight: 700,\n          color: \"#111113\",\n        }}\n      >\n        Releases\n      </h1>\n\n      {/* Filters */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n        <select\n          value={projectFilter}\n          onChange={(e) => {\n            setProjectFilter(e.target.value);\n            setPage(1);\n          }}\n          className=\"appearance-none rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 outline-none transition-shadow\"\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#111113\",\n            border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n          }}\n          onFocus={(e) =>\n            (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = \"0 0 0 2px #e8601a40\")\n          }\n          onBlur={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = \"none\")}\n        >\n          <option value=\"all\">All Projects</option>\n          {projectsData?.data.map((p: Project) => (\n            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>\n              {p.name}\n            </option>\n          ))}\n        </select>\n\n        <label className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none\">\n          <span style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}>\n            Show excluded\n          </span>\n          <button\n            role=\"switch\"\n            aria-checked={showExcluded}\n            onClick={() => {\n              const next = !showExcluded;\n              setShowExcluded(next);\n              setPage(1);\n              const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n              if (next) params.delete(\"show_excluded\");\n              else params.set(\"show_excluded\", \"false\");\n              window.history.replaceState({}, \"\", `?${params.toString()}`);\n            }}\n            className=\"relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 items-center rounded-full transition-colors\"\n            style={{ backgroundColor: showExcluded ? \"#e8601a\" : \"#d1d5db\" }}\n          >\n            <span\n              className=\"inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform\"\n              style={{ transform: showExcluded ? \"translateX(18px)\" : \"translateX(3px)\" }}\n            />\n          </button>\n        </label>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Table card */}\n      <div\n        className=\"overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-white\"\n        style={{ border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\" }}\n      >\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div\n            className=\"py-16 text-center\"\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"13px\",\n              color: \"#6b7280\",\n            }}\n          >\n            Loading...\n          </div>\n        ) : releases.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"py-16 text-center\">\n            <p\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n                fontStyle: \"italic\",\n                fontSize: \"15px\",\n                color: \"#9ca3af\",\n              }}\n            >\n              No releases ingested yet\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <table className=\"w-full\">\n            <thead>\n              <tr style={{ borderBottom: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\", backgroundColor: \"#fafaf9\" }}>\n                {[\"Project\", \"Provider\", \"Repository\", \"Version\", \"Released\", \"Age\", \"Report\", \"\"].map(\n                  (col) => (\n                    <th\n                      key={col}\n                      className=\"px-4 py-3 text-left\"\n                      style={{\n                        fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        fontSize: \"11px\",\n                        fontWeight: 600,\n                        textTransform: \"uppercase\" as const,\n                        letterSpacing: \"0.08em\",\n                        color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {col}\n                    </th>\n                  )\n                )}\n              </tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody>\n              {releases.map((release) => (\n                <tr\n                  key={release.id}\n                  className={`transition-colors ${release.excluded ? '' : 'hover:bg-[#fafaf9]'}`}\n                  style={{\n                    borderBottom: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n                    opacity: release.excluded ? 0.45 : 1,\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {/* Project */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    {release.project_id ? (\n                      <Link\n                        href={`/projects/${release.project_id}`}\n                        className=\"hover:underline\"\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          fontSize: \"13px\",\n                          color: \"#111113\",\n                          fontWeight: 500,\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {release.project_name ?? \"\\u2014\"}\n                      </Link>\n                    ) : (\n                      <span\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          fontSize: \"13px\",\n                          color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {\"\\u2014\"}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Provider */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    {release.provider ? (\n                      <ProviderBadge provider={release.provider} />\n                    ) : (\n                      <span\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          fontSize: \"13px\",\n                          color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {\"\\u2014\"}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Repository */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    <span\n                      style={{\n                        fontFamily: \"'JetBrains Mono', monospace\",\n                        fontSize: \"12px\",\n                        color: \"#374151\",\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {release.repository ?? release.source_id}\n                    </span>\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Version */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    <Link href={`/releases/${release.id}`}>\n                      <VersionChip version={release.version} />\n                    </Link>\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Released date */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    <span\n                      style={{\n                        fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        fontSize: \"13px\",\n                        color: \"#6b7280\",\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {release.released_at\n                        ? new Date(release.released_at).toLocaleDateString()\n                        : \"\\u2014\"}\n                    </span>\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Age */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    <span\n                      style={{\n                        fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        fontSize: \"13px\",\n                        color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      {timeAgo(release.released_at ?? release.created_at)}\n                    </span>\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Report */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    {release.semantic_release_status === \"completed\" && release.semantic_release_id && release.project_id ? (\n                      <Link\n                        href={`/projects/${release.project_id}/semantic-releases/${release.semantic_release_id}`}\n                        className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#eff6ff]\"\n                        style={{\n                          color: \"#1d4ed8\",\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        <FileText size={13} />\n                        Report\n                      </Link>\n                    ) : release.semantic_release_status === \"pending\" || release.semantic_release_status === \"processing\" ? (\n                      <span\n                        className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px]\"\n                        style={{ color: \"#f59e0b\", fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\" }}\n                      >\n                        <Loader2 size={13} className=\"animate-spin\" />\n                        {release.semantic_release_status}\n                      </span>\n                    ) : release.project_id && !release.excluded ? (\n                      <button\n                        onClick={() => handleTrigger(release.project_id!, release.version)}\n                        disabled={triggeringVersion === release.version}\n                        className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#fef3c7] disabled:opacity-50\"\n                        style={{\n                          color: \"#92400e\",\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        }}\n                        title=\"Generate report\"\n                      >\n                        <Sparkles size={13} />\n                        Analyze\n                      </button>\n                    ) : (\n                      <span\n                        style={{\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                          fontSize: \"13px\",\n                          color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        {\"\\u2014\"}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </td>\n\n                  {/* Provider link */}\n                  <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                    {release.provider && release.repository && (() => {\n                      const url = getProviderUrl(release.provider, release.repository, release.version);\n                      return url ? (\n                        <a\n                          href={url}\n                          target=\"_blank\"\n                          rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                          className=\"inline-flex items-center transition-colors hover:opacity-70\"\n                          style={{ color: \"#9ca3af\" }}\n                          title=\"View on provider\"\n                        >\n                          <ExternalLink size={14} />\n                        </a>\n                      ) : null;\n                    })()}\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n              ))}\n            </tbody>\n          </table>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Pagination */}\n      {total > 0 && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <span\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"13px\",\n              color: \"#9ca3af\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {startRow}&ndash;{endRow} of {total}\n          </span>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <button\n              disabled={page <= 1}\n              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}\n              className=\"rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                fontSize: \"13px\",\n                color: \"#374151\",\n                border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n            >\n              Previous\n            </button>\n            <button\n              disabled={page >= totalPages}\n              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}\n              className=\"rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#fafaf9] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                fontSize: \"13px\",\n                color: \"#374151\",\n                border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n              }}\n            >\n              Next\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

All 8 tasks completed successfully. Here's a summary of what was done:

### Backend
- **`internal/models/release.go`** — Added 3 fields: `SemanticReleaseID`, `SemanticReleaseStatus`, `SemanticReleaseUrgency`
- **`internal/api/pgstore.go`** — Modified all 4 release query methods (8 SQL queries) to `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` on `semantic_releases`, selecting the most recent SR for each release by matching `(project_id, version)`. Added the 3 new fields to all `Scan()` calls.

### Frontend
- **`web/lib/api/types.ts`** — Added 3 optional fields to `Release` interface
- **`web/app/releases/page.tsx`** — Added "Report" column showing: completed → FileText link, pending/processing → spinner, none → Sparkles "Analyze" button that triggers agent run. Added SSE revalidation for real-time updates.
- **`web/app/projects/page.tsx`** — Removed separate "Semantic Releases" FlowSection from cards. Added inline FileText/Loader2 indicators after version chips in both `ProjectFlowCard` and `ProjectCompactRow`.
- **`web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`** — Removed "Semantic Releases" nav item and `Brain` import
- **`web/app/semantic-releases/page.tsx`** — Replaced with redirect to `/releases`

### Verification
- `go vet ./...` — clean
- `go test ./...` — all packages pass

> AGENT

The SQL looks correct on paper. The issue is likely that `COALESCE` returns `''` (empty string) when there's no match, and the Go model has `omitempty` — so empty strings are serialized as `""` in JSON but the frontend checks for specific status values. Let me check what the API actually returns by looking at how the frontend handles it.

> AGENT

The frontend logic looks correct. The problem is in the Go model — `omitempty` on strings means empty strings (`""`) are omitted from JSON output. Since `COALESCE` returns `''` for unmatched rows, Go scans them as `""`, and `omitempty` drops them from JSON. That's fine for the "no SR" case.

But for matched rows, the status should be `"completed"` etc. Let me check if maybe the issue is that `p.id` is NULL for some releases (sources without a project), causing the lateral join to never match. Let me also check the actual database to see what's happening.

Actually, wait — I think I found it. The `omitempty` tag is the problem for the **matched** case too. Let me re-check the model.

> TOOL

```json
{
  "command": "PGPASSWORD=postgres psql -h localhost -U postgres -d releaseguard -c \"\nSELECT r.version, p.name,\n       COALESCE(sr_info.id::text,'') as sr_id,\n       COALESCE(sr_info.status,'') as sr_status,\n       COALESCE(sr_info.urgency,'') as sr_urgency\nFROM releases r\nLEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\nLEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\nLEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n    SELECT sr.id, sr.status, sr.report->>'urgency' AS urgency\n    FROM semantic_releases sr\n    WHERE sr.project_id = p.id AND sr.version = r.version\n    ORDER BY sr.created_at DESC LIMIT 1\n) sr_info ON true\nORDER BY r.created_at DESC\nLIMIT 15;\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test the exact lateral join query"
}
```

roundtrip view), `diff_jsx` (structural diff), `describe` (semantic role, visual style, layout, design issues)\n- AI visual verification \u2014 `export_image` returns image content to the model for bindings to `.fig` files \u2014 previously lost on save (#65)\n- Text nodes created via MCP now render in Figma \u2014 emit `derivedTextData` with font metadata and layout size (#64)\n- Double-click on layer tree no longer toggles expand/collapse \u2014 use the chevron instead\n- Page rename input matches layer rename styling\n- Fix `w=\"fill\"`/`h=\"fill\"` in JSX renderer \u2014 now direction-aware based on parent flex axis\n- Fix text auto-resize defaulting to fixed 100\u00d7100 \u2014 text without explicit width uses `WIDTH_AND_HEIGHT`\n- Fix `clipsContent` not propagated to Yoga \u2014 frames with clip enabled now set `Overflow.Hidden`\n- Fix `COUNTER_ALIGN_MAP` mapping stretch to `MIN` instead of `STRETCH`\n- Fix JSX export omitting x/y for absolute-positioned children\n- Fix JSX export ignoring `textAutoResize` for text sizing\n- Fix drag terminating on mouseleave \u2014 drags now continue outside the canvas\n- Fix `export_image` stack overflow on large nodes \u2014 chunked base64 encoding\n- Undo support for auto-layout reorder, layer tree reorder, and drag reparent\n- Page snapshot undo for AI tool mutations\n- Fix collab sync for same-parent Performance\n\n- Event-driven rendering and component sync \u2014 `SceneGraph` emits typed events on mutations; `requestRender()` calls reduced from 94 to 22, component instance sync uses microtask batching with deduplication\n- Replace `structuredClone` with typed copy helpers for fills, strokes, effects, and style runs (~24\u00d7 faster in hot paths)\n- Filter .fig unzip to only decompress auto-layout\n- AI tools run `computeAllLayouts` after execution \u2014 layout updates immediately\n- Enhanced AI system prompt with full JSX prop reference and verification workflow\n- Chat panel preserves messages when toggling UI visibility\n- SceneGraph event bus (nanoevents) \u2014 `node:created`, `node:updated`, `node:deleted`, `node:reparented`, `node:reordered` events replace monkey-patching in collab sync and manual render invalidation\n- Replace esbuild-wasm (14 MB) with sucrase (201 KB) for JSX transform \u2014 `buildComponent()` and `renderJSX()` now synchronous and debug toolbar for copying chat logs\n- Auto-layout icons in layer tree \u2014 vertical (rows), horizontal (columns), and grid icons for auto-layout frames; components keep their purple diamond\n- > AGENT

The problem: `triggeringVersion` is cleared in `finally` right after `agent.triggerRun()` resolves, so the button goes back to "Analyze" before the SWR revalidation completes and returns the `pending` status from the backend. The fix is to keep `triggeringVersion` set until the revalidation brings back the new status.

> TOOL

  "new_string": "  /* Trigger agent run — keep spinner until SWR revalidation brings back the new status */\n  const handleTrigger = async (projectId: string, version: string) => {\n    setTriggeringVersion(version);\n    try {\n      await agent.triggerRun(projectId, version);\n      await revalidateReleases();\n    } catch {\n      setTriggeringVersion(null);\n    }\n  };"
}

> AGENT

There's an agent run with status `running` and no semantic release yet. The problem is clear: when an agent run is in progress but hasn't created a semantic release row yet, the `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` on `semantic_releases` returns nothing. The release row has no SR status.

The fix is to also join on `agent_runs` to detect in-progress analysis. Let me check the agent_runs schema.
