> AGENT

I'll replace the lateral subquery in all 8 places to use a `UNION ALL` that falls back to `agent_runs` when no semantic release exists. This way, if there's an in-progress agent run for that `(project_id, version)`, the status will show as `processing`.

The new lateral subquery:
```sql
LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT sr.id, sr.status, sr.report->>'urgency' AS urgency
    FROM semantic_releases sr
    WHERE sr.project_id = p.id AND sr.version = r.version
    ORDER BY sr.created_at DESC LIMIT 1
    UNION ALL
    SELECT NULL::uuid, ar.status, ''
    FROM agent_runs ar
    WHERE ar.project_id = p.id AND ar.version = r.version
      AND ar.status IN ('pending', 'running')
    ORDER BY ar.created_at DESC LIMIT 1
    LIMIT 1
) sr_info ON true
```

Wait — `UNION ALL` with separate `ORDER BY` and `LIMIT` per branch won't work that way in PostgreSQL. Let me use a cleaner approach with a subquery that prefers SR over agent_run.

Actually, the simplest approach: use two separate subqueries combined. But that's complex. Let me just wrap it properly.

  "new_string": "func (s *PgStore) GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error) {\n\tvar rel models.Release\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT r.id, r.source_id, r.version, COALESCE(r.raw_data,'{}'), r.released_at, r.created_at,\n\t\t        COALESCE(p.id::text,''), COALESCE(p.name,''), COALESCE(s.provider,''), COALESCE(s.repository,''),\n\t\t        COALESCE(sr_info.id::text,''), COALESCE(sr_info.status,''), COALESCE(sr_info.urgency,'')\n\t\t FROM releases r\n\t\t LEFT JOIN sources s ON r.source_id = s.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id\n\t\t LEFT JOIN LATERAL (\n\t\t     (SELECT sr.id, sr.status, sr.report->>'urgency' AS urgency, 0 AS priority\n\t\t      FROM semantic_releases sr\n\t\t      WHERE sr.project_id = p.id AND sr.version = r.version\n\t\t      ORDER BY sr.created_at DESC LIMIT 1)\n\t\t     UNION ALL\n\t\t     (SELECT NULL::uuid, 'processing', '', 1\n\t\t      FROM agent_runs ar\n\t\t      WHERE ar.project_id = p.id AND ar.version = r.version\n\t\t        AND ar.status IN ('pending', 'running')\n\t\t      LIMIT 1)\n\t\t     ORDER BY priority LIMIT 1\n\t\t ) sr_info ON true\n\t\t WHERE r.id = $1`, id,\n\t).Scan(&rel.ID, &rel.SourceID, &rel.Version, &rel.RawData, &rel.ReleasedAt, &rel.CreatedAt,\n\t\t&rel.ProjectID, &rel.ProjectName, &rel.Provider, &rel.Repository,\n\t\t&rel.SemanticReleaseID, &rel.SemanticReleaseStatus, &rel.SemanticReleaseUrgency)"

> AGENT

The frontend already checks for `"processing"` status. The fix is complete:

**What changed:**

- **Backend** (`internal/api/pgstore.go`): The lateral subquery now uses a `UNION ALL` with `agent_runs` as a fallback. If no `semantic_releases` row exists but there's an active agent run (`pending` or `running`) for that `(project_id, version)`, it returns `status = 'processing'`. The semantic release row always takes priority (via `priority` column).

- **Frontend** (`web/app/releases/page.tsx`): The spinner condition already handles `"processing"` status. Additionally, `triggeringVersion` now shows "analyzing" immediately on click, and the SSE handler clears it on completion.

The Go server needs to be rebuilt (`make run`) for the backend changes to take effect. After that, refreshing the page while an agent is running will show the spinner.

   241→        <button
   242→          onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}

   244→          style={{

   249→        >
   250→          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   251→          Add Source
   252→        </button>
   253→      </div>
   254→
   255→      {/* Releases flow */}
   256→      {releases.length > 0 && (
   257→        <FlowSection label="Releases" moreHref={`/releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   258→          {releases.map((r) => {
   259→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   260→            const matchingSr = srItems.find((sr) => sr.version === r.version);
   261→            return (
   262→              <span key={r.id} className="inline-flex items-baseline mr-2.5">
   263→                <Link
   264→                  href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   265→                  className={r.excluded ? "" : "text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"}
   266→                  style={{
   267→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   268→                    fontSize: "12px",
   269→                    ...(r.excluded ? { color: "#c4c4c0" } : {}),
   270→                  }}
   271→                >
   272→                  {r.version}
   273→                </Link>
   274→                {!r.excluded && matchingSr?.status === "completed" && (
   275→                  <Link
   276→                    href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${matchingSr.id}`}
   277→                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded px-1 py-0.5 ml-1 text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-[#dbeafe]"
   278→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#eff6ff", color: "#1d4ed8", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   279→                    title="View report"
   280→                  >
   281→                    <FileText size={10} />
   282→                    Report
   283→                  </Link>
   284→                )}
   285→                {!r.excluded && (matchingSr?.status === "pending" || matchingSr?.status === "processing") && (
   286→                  <span
   287→                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded px-1 py-0.5 ml-1 text-[10px]"
   288→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#fef9c3", color: "#92400e", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   289→                  >
   290→                    <Loader2 size={10} className="animate-spin" />
   291→                  </span>
   292→                )}
   293→                {src && (
   294→                  <span
   295→                    className="text-[11px] ml-1 hidden sm:inline"
   296→                    style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#9ca3af" }}
   297→                  >
   298→                    ({src.repository.split("/").pop()})
   299→                  </span>
   300→                )}
   301→                <span
   302→                  className="text-[11px] ml-1"
   303→                  style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#c4c4c0" }}
   304→                >
   305→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   306→                </span>
   307→              </span>
   308→            );
   309→          })}
   310→        </FlowSection>
   311→      )}
   312→
   313→      {/* Add Source dialog */}
   314→      <Dialog open={sourceCreateOpen} onOpenChange={setSourceCreateOpen}>
   315→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   316→          <DialogHeader>
   317→            <DialogTitle>Add Source</DialogTitle>
   318→          </DialogHeader>
   319→          <SourceForm
   320→            title="Add Source"
   321→            projectId={project.id}
   322→            onSubmit={async (input) => {
   323→              const res = await sourcesApi.create(project.id, input);
   324→              if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   325→            }}
   326→            onSuccess={() => {
   327→              setSourceCreateOpen(false);
   328→              mutateSources();
   329→            }}
   330→            onCancel={() => setSourceCreateOpen(false)}
   331→          />
   332→        </DialogContent>
   333→      </Dialog>
   334→
   335→      {/* Edit Source dialog */}
   336→      <Dialog
   337→        open={!!editingSource}
   338→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingSource(null); }}
   339→      >
   340→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   341→          <DialogHeader>
   342→            <DialogTitle>Edit Source</DialogTitle>
   343→          </DialogHeader>
   344→          {editingSource && (
   345→            <SourceForm
   346→              key={editingSource.id}
   347→              title="Edit Source"
   348→              initial={editingSource}
   349→              onSubmit={async (input) => {
   350→                await sourcesApi.update(editingSource.id, input);
   351→              }}
   352→              onSuccess={() => {
   353→                setEditingSource(null);
   354→                mutateSources();
   355→              }}
   356→              onCancel={() => setEditingSource(null)}
   357→            />
   358→          )}
   359→        </DialogContent>

I've read the complete file `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/page.tsx`. Here's a summary of what the file contains:

## Overview
This is a Next.js React component (client-side) that displays a projects page for the ReleaseBeacon application. It's a comprehensive projects management interface with multiple viewing modes and filtering capabilities.

## Key Components

**Main Component: `ProjectsPage`** (lines 493-697)
- Manages project list display with search, sorting, and pagination
- Supports two view modes: "cards" and "compact" list
- Features a search bar, sort dropdown (by "updated", "added", or "name"), and view toggle buttons
- Includes a "New Project" button to create projects
- Displays 12 items per page with pagination controls

**Supporting Components:**

1. **`ProjectFlowCard`** (lines 150-363) - Card view for projects showing:
   - Project name and description
   - Source chips with enable/disable indicators and last polled times
   - Recent releases with status badges ("Report" for completed semantic analysis)
   - Dialogs for adding/editing sources

2. **`ProjectCompactRow`** (lines 367-489) - Compact list view showing:
   - Project name with logo
   - Latest release version
   - Urgency badge (critical/high/medium/low)
   - Summary of the latest semantic release
   - Color-coded urgency indicators

3. **`FlowSection`** (lines 44-146) - Responsive overflow handling component:
   - Measures text content to fit within 2 lines
   - Shows "more..." link when items overflow
   - Uses ResizeObserver for responsive measurements

4. **`ProjectCardLogo`** (lines 31-36) - Helper component that displays a project logo

## Features
- Real-time data fetching using SWR for projects, releases, sources, and semantic releases
- Responsive UI with conditional rendering based on loading states
- Dialog modals for creating and editing projects/sources
- Integration with API client methods for CRUD operations
- Styling using Tailwind CSS with custom inline styles

The file is 698 lines total and handles the complete project listing, filtering, sorting, and management interface.
